### Sheffi Paz, prominent Tel Aviv anti-illegal immigration activist, dies at 74
- **Source**: JPost.com - The Jerusalem Post - All News from the Middle East, Israel, and the Jewish World

### Algeria to send troops to Niger in first foreign deployment in over 50 years
- **Source**: France 24 - International breaking news, top stories and headlines
- **Author**: FRANCE 24
- **Date**: 2026-10-26 10:10
- **Link**: https://www.france24.com/en/africa/20261026-algeria-to-send-troops-to-niger-in-first-foreign-deployment-in-over-50-years
- **Summary**: AlgeriaΓÇÖs parliament on Sunday approved a resolution to send troops to Niger, making the first time Algerian soldiers will be deployed abroad since the 1973 Arab-Israeli war. Niger faces persistent attacks by armed groups, including near its border with Algeria, and the deployment comes as Algiers and Niamey are building the Trans-Saharan Gas Pipeline after decades of delays.

### Israeli drones burn Gaza refugee camp, displacing about 800 Palestinian men, women and children
- **Source**: Section Feed

- **Summary**: Israeli drones burn Gaza refugee camp, displacing about 800 Palestinian men, women and children A fire started by Israeli quadcopter drones destroyed tents in Gaza CityΓÇÖs Shujayea neighbourhood, leaving about 800 Palestinians displaced, according to the Palestinian Public Works Ministry. The ministry said the drones ignited the blaze at about 8pm local time (17:00 GMT) on Sunday. Flames continued to burn beyond midnight, consuming shelters that housed mainly women, children and elderly people. It appealed for international assistance to supply replacement tents.

### Oil spill off UAE widens, satellite images show, prompting more beach closures
- **Source**: AL-MONITOR: The Pulse of The Middle East
- **Author**: 
- **Date**: 2026-10-26 09:46
- **Link**: https://www.al-monitor.com/originals/2026/10/oil-spill-uae-widens-satellite-images-show-prompting-more-beach-closures
- **Summary**: By Vinaya K and Federico MaccioniDUBAI, Oct 26 (Reuters) - An oil spill from a damaged tanker off the coast of the United Arab Emirates has widened, satellite images showed, triggering more beach closures and raising environmental concerns.Images from the European Union's Copernicus satellite observation network dated October 24 show the oil spill has widened over the last few days and is reaching close to the shores of the northern Umm Al Quwain emirate, with dark layers of oil spreading at sea and alongits coastline.

### Brazil's Lula wins historic fourth term
- **Source**: France 24 - International breaking news, top stories and headlines
- **Author**: FRANCE24
- **Date**: 2026-10-26 09:44
- **Link**: https://www.france24.com/en/brazil-s-lula-wins-historic-fourth-term
- **Summary**: Brazilian President Luiz Inacio Lula da Silva, nearly 81 years old, clinched a fourth term in a tight runoff against Flavio Bolsonaro on Sunday (October 26). The leftist leader won 51.7% of the vote, compared to 48.2% for the son of former president Jair Bolsonaro, official results showed. Bolsonaro has refused to accept the loss and said he would seek to annul the vote.

### Brazil's Lula seals re-election in surprise win

- **Date**: 2026-10-26 09:33

- **Summary**: Brazil's President Luiz Inacio Lula da Silva won an unprecedented fourth term in a tight runoff against Flavio Bolsonaro on Sunday (October 25). They're about France Submitted by Soumaya Ghannoushi on Fri, 10/23/2026 - 16:13 The country's crisis is deep, structural and entrenched, and it will grow more severe if the far right wins the next election French police officers stand near a fire set by protesters during a demonstration in Montpellier, southern France, on 17 October 2026 (Sylvain Thomas/AFP) Off The uprising sweeping through Paris and cities across France this autumn, at times descending into violence, looting and destruction, has laid bare the depth of the crisis engulfing the country under President Emmanuel Macron. It is no passing outburst of anger. It is closer to May 1968, when a student revolt grew into a national upheaval whose legacy still shapes France today. If MacronΓÇÖs presidency could be defined by a single word, it would be anger. From the Yellow Vests, to the strikes against pension reforms, to the riots in the suburbs and the demonstrations of today, France has lived in a state of almost perpetual agitation. Macron did not create all of the countryΓÇÖs ills, but he has aggravated them with his arrogance and condescension towards the French people, particularly citizens of North African and African descent, and even towards FranceΓÇÖs African partners. The revolt is rooted in a suffocating domestic crisis: declining purchasing power, stagnant wages, rising prices and spreading poverty. Young people, along with the working and lower-middle classes, have borne t...

- **Date**: 2026-10-26 08:41

- **Summary**: Oliver Farry is pleased to welcome Dr Felipe Krause, Political Scientist, former Diplomat and Departmental Lecturer in Latin American Studies at the Oxford School of Global and Area Studies. Beneath Lula's electoral triumph lies a more complex political reality: the Brazilian right has consolidated its influence across state governments and legislative institutions, raising fundamental questions about the balance of power and the future of progressive governance. 'There is more need for economic measures that support growing middle class,' Dr. Krause explains. 'He's going to be working to keep things from eroding rather than actually managing to advance on many of these important agendas such as the environment."

### For Palestinians, Israeli election will only determine the pace of dispossession
- **Source**: Section Feed
- **Author**: Emile Badarin
- **Date**: 2026-10-26 08:32

- **Summary**: For Palestinians, Israeli election will only determine the pace of dispossession Submitted by Emile Badarin on Wed, 10/21/2026 - 15:54 As in settler-colonial societies across history, the real issues of indigenous rights are left off the ballot People walk past an election poster for Gadi Eisenkot, the former military chief and leader of the Yashar party, in Ramat Gan, Israel, on 15 October 2026 (Jack Guez/AFP) Off IsraelΓÇÖs general election on 27 October is widely being framed as a referendum on Prime Minister Benjamin Netanyahu. Polls suggest that neither NetanyahuΓÇÖs bloc, nor the opposition bloc of four Jewish-led parties, will win a majority in the Knesset, IsraelΓÇÖs parliament. Arab parties, representing 20 percent of the citizenry, remain outside the arithmetic of government. Their exclusion reveals much about the political terms on which this election is being fought. Across history, the settler-colonial democracies that most thoroughly dispossessed indigenous peoples - the United States, Canada and Australia - have held elections that advanced this process. Because elimination and land seizures underpinned their existence, they never put these issues on the ballot; voters chose only who would do it most effectively and rapidly. Similarly, Israelis are not choosing a direction in the coming election. They never have. They are choosing the pace of ethnic cleansing, genocide and dispossession of the Palestinian people. Indeed, IsraelΓÇÖs election campaign follows a famili...

### IsraelΓÇÖs Netanyahu clashes with army chief over Iran-related military action: Report
- **Source**: Section Feed
- **Author**: Mera Aladam
- **Date**: 2026-10-26 08:03
- **Link**: https://www.middleeasteye.net/live-blog/live-blog-update/israels-netanyahu-clashes-army-chief-over-iran-related-military-action
- **Summary**: IsraelΓÇÖs Netanyahu clashes with army chief over Iran-related military action: Report Israeli Prime Minister Benjamin Netanyahu and Army Chief of Staff Eyal Zamir reportedly had an "unprecedented confrontation" over a proposed military operation that could trigger a war with Iran ahead of the elections on Tuesday. According to Israeli Channel 12 reported Sunday, Zamir has resisted carrying out the military action - that remains unspecified - and requested signed documentation from Netanyahu regarding legal authorisation for it. "This relates to an action that could lead to a flare-up with Iran, at a sensitive and dramatic moment, creating this [potential] miscalculation," a military source was quoted as saying. The report also cited another source that said there is military concerns that the political leadership could exploit tensions with Iran for "political benefit". Channel 12 added that the hostility between Netanyahu and Zamir were evident at discussions on Sunday at the Palmahim Airbase, where the atmosphere in the room was described by a source as "extremely sour".

- **Summary**: Early on the morning of July 16, 1808, Captain-General Francisco Javier Casta├▒os of the Army of Andalusia launched a surprise attack against the divided forces of Emperor Napoleon BonaparteΓÇÖs Army of the Gironde. After three grueling days of combat, French Gen. Pierre Dupont requested a truce and opened negotiations outside the village of Bail├⌐n. Beginning on July 22, 17,000 French soldiers ΓÇö encircled, exhausted, and out of supplies ΓÇö surrendered their arms, their flags, and three imperial eagles to the Spanish ΓÇö men who had first shouldered a musket scarcely eight weeks earlier. ### Iran's Bagheri confirms top officials from Mecca Pact countries' visit to Tehran
- **Source**: Section Feed
- **Author**: Mera Aladam
- **Date**: 2026-10-26 07:29
- **Link**: https://www.middleeasteye.net/live-blog/live-blog-update/irans-bagheri-confirms-top-officials-mecca-pact-countries-visit-tehran
- **Summary**: Iran's Bagheri confirms top officials from Mecca Pact countries' visit to Tehran The deputy secretary of the Supreme National Security Council, Ali Bagheri, announced the visit of senior officials from Saudi Arabia, Pakistan and Turkey to Tehran. Bagheri said the meeting is aimed at discussing regional security, and will include Saudi Defence Minister Khalid bin Salman, Pakistani Army Commander General Asim Munir, and Turkish Intelligence Chief Ibrahim Kalin. "The Islamic Republic of Iran's model of defence based on internal and domestic capabilities has become a successful model for building national security," he said. "On the other hand, Iran's strategic policy is to ensure regional security through regional countries and confront any foreign interference and influence, led by the American and Zionist regimes." Bagheri described the initiative as the "fruit of the heroic efforts and struggles of the armed forces and the Iranian nation", expressing his hopes that it would lead to a "region with stability and peace and on the path of progress".

### Lula defeats pro-Israel challenger Flavio Bolsonaro in Brazil elections
- **Source**: Section Feed

- **Date**: 2026-10-26 07:21

- **Summary**: Lula defeats pro-Israel challenger Flavio Bolsonaro in Brazil elections Submitted by Mera Aladam on Mon, 10/26/2026 - 05:39 The incumbent secured his fourth term as leader after falling behind his far-right challenger in the first round of voting Brazil's President Luiz Inacio Lula da Silva gestures as he addresses supporters after his victory in the presidential runoff election, at Paulista Avenue, in Sao Paulo, Brazil, 26 October (Reuters/Amanda Perobelli) Off Brazilian President Luiz Inacio "Lula" da Silva secured his fourth non-consecutive presidential term late on Sunday, in a historic win against right-wing pro-Israel Senator Flavio Bolsonaro. Lula won the tight race by 51.78 percent of valid votes against 48.22 percent for Bolsonaro, according to Brazil's Superior Electoral Court (TSE). Heavy rain and technical issues had affected voting across the vast Latin American nation, leading the country to keep open polls for two extra hours past 7pm local time (10pm GMT). The defeated presidential candidate Bolsonaro announced he would call for the results to be annulled, citing the two-hour extension to voting given by the Supreme Court. "We will engage our legal team and seek to have these elections annulled," he said. "I will not accept that the rules of the game have been arbitrarily changed - once again - by LulaΓÇÖs friends on the Federal Supreme Court." Bolsonaro's statement comes despite reports that the request for the extension was called for by both candidates, ci...

- **Summary**: Lana Nusseibeh has been the face of a disastrous Emirates policy that has prolonged war and death. It is a scandal that she is even being consideredOver the past few years, you can plot the plunge into global instability not only through the unchecked assaults on international law and civilian life, but through the decline and undermining of the authority and credibility of international institutions. The latest level in this spiral is the proposed appointment of Lana Nusseibeh of the United Arab Emirates (UAE) as the UN secretary general. Her bid has been rapidly gaining momentum and endorsements. And it has been met with outrage among the Sudanese.Nusseibeh is the minister of state at the UAE and a former UAE ambassador to the UN. For the past three years, the country has been connected to ΓÇô bear with me, this will be a long sentence ΓÇô supporting the Rapid Support Forces (RSF) militia in Sudan, facilitating mercenaries to fight alongside the militia (which is accused of ethnic cleansing, crimes against humanity and sexual violence), and appropriating international aid infrastructure to smuggle weapons into the country. ### Lula wins reelection in Brazil, securing fourth presidential term
- **Source**: Section Feed
- **Author**: Mera Aladam
- **Date**: 2026-10-26 06:25
- **Link**: https://www.middleeasteye.net/live-blog/live-blog-update/lula-wins-reelection-brazil-securing-fourth-presidential-term
- **Summary**: Lula wins reelection in Brazil, securing fourth presidential term Leftist President Luiz Inacio Lula da Silva has defeated right-wing Senator Flavio Bolsonaro by more than three points in the second round of BrazilΓÇÖs presidential election. A video published by LulaΓÇÖs campaign shows him celebrating with First Lady Ros├óngela da Silva and allies after his victory was confirmed by BrazilΓÇÖs electoral court. The win secures his fourth non-consecutive term in office. Bolsonaro has refused to concede defeat, calling the move to extend voting hours unprecedented, though both he and Lula made the request, and has called for the results to be annulled. Leftist President Luiz Inacio Lula da Silva has defeated right-wing Senator Flavio Bolsonaro by more than three points in the second round of BrazilΓÇÖs presidential election. https://t.co/NoLzGnJQ83 ΓÇö Middle East Eye (@MiddleEastEye) October 26, 2026

- **Summary**: Photographer Zeinab Faraj, 21, found herself buried under rubble beside a dying colleague while reporting in Lebanon. She had never expected to the target of an Israeli missile attack.It was mid-April and after a month of deadly airstrikes across Lebanon, Israel agreed to a ceasefire. Much of the bombing had focused on southern Lebanon, where entire villages had been demolished. But with the truce, people were hopeful of being able to return to their homes or to see what remained.Zeinab Faraj, a 21-year-old photojournalist, decided to join Amal Khalil, 42, a newspaper reporter in Lebanon, on a trip to an area in the south of the country to document the aftermath of war. The two had worked together on a number of assignments after Khalil had seen FarajΓÇÖs work on social media and contacted her. Continue reading...

- **Author**:

- **Link**: https://www.al-monitor.com/originals/2026/10/houthis-say-they-attacked-saudi-oil-pumping-stations-riyadh-airport-fighting
- **Summary**: Oct 26 (Reuters) - Yemen's Iran-aligned Houthis said on Monday they had attacked Saudi Arabia's East-West crude oil pumping stations and Riyadh airport, as rising hostilities choke oil supplies andthreaten to erupt into a larger conflict.The Saudi-led coalition in Yemen said earlier it had destroyed two ballistic missile launchers in the capital Sanaa, including one used to fire a missile towards Riyadh that the coalition said it had intercepted.There was no immediate Saudi confirmation of the Houthi attacks.

- **Summary**: By AbdelHadi Ramahi and Yomna EhabAL-JUDAYYDA, Jordan, CAIRO, Oct 26 (Reuters) - Jordanian security forces arrested three armed suspects after a shootout on Monday in connection with an attack on a church that killed nine people in the US-allied country, state television reported.Friday's attack during a funeral at a Greek Orthodox church in the village of Hamoud, about 120 km (75 miles) southwest of the capital, Amman, also wounded 40 others, alarming a Christian community.

- **Summary**: Hebron is under full Israeli siege, mayor says Israeli forces have sealed off all entrances to Hebron in the occupied West Bank and forcibly displaced around 2,200 Palestinians from their homes, Mayor Yousef Jaabari told Middle East Eye on Saturday evening. ΓÇ£Hebron is now a disaster zone. The occupation has closed all entrances to the city. No one can enter or leave,ΓÇ¥ Jaabari said, accusing Israel of imposing collective punishment following a deadly shooting on Friday. Two suspected Palestinians opened fire on Israeli troops at a checkpoint in northern Hebron early in the day near the Farsh al-Hawa checkpoint west of Hebron, killing two soldiers and wounding three others. Jaabari said Israeli forces forcibly evicted around 2,200 people, including elderly people, women and children, from Deir Bahala, al-Jalda and surrounding neighbourhoods. The affected area spans approximately 330 dunams and contains more than 490 housing units. Residents told MEE that soldiers gave families five minutes to leave while videos posted on social media showed Palestinian lorry drivers working together to move goods and supplies out of factories ahead of their demolition by Israeli forces. Bolsonaro has refused to accept the loss and said he would seek to annul the vote.

- **Summary**: Israeli forces release Palestinian 'doctor of the poor ' with visible signs of abuse Israeli forces released Palestinian doctor Mazen al-Rantisi, 71, from Ofer prison on Sunday after nearly four months in detention, with visible signs of physical abuse on his body. In a photo circulated on social media after his release, the physician appeared gaunt, with a prominent red wound on his forehead. Popularly described as the ΓÇ£doctor of the poorΓÇ¥ for waiving fees, supplying medicines and operating a clinic that became a refuge for low-income Palestinians, al-Rantisi was detained in a pre-dawn raid on his home in Ramallah in the occupied West Bank on 21 June. Prosecutors later indicted him for serving as chairman of the board of the Union of Health Work Committees, a Palestinian non-profit that provides medical care to thousands of Palestinians each year. Read more: Israeli forces release Palestinian 'doctor of the poor ' with visible signs of abuse RantisiΓÇÖs detention prompted widespread condemnation (Screengrab/X)

### Billionaire Trump backer says Ben-Gvir, Smotrich should not serve in next Israeli government
- **Source**: AL-MONITOR: The Pulse of The Middle East
- **Author**: 
- **Date**: 2026-10-25 15:46
- **Link**: https://www.al-monitor.com/originals/2026/10/billionaire-trump-backer-says-ben-gvir-smotrich-should-not-serve-next-israeli
- **Summary**: By Steven Scheer and Alexander CornwellJERUSALEM, Oct 25 (Reuters) - Billionaire American investor Bill Ackman on Sunday said that Itamar Ben-Gvir and Bezalel Smotrich should not serve in Israel's next government, saying the two far-right politicians had caused enormous damage to the country's reputation.The public intervention by the prominent hedge fund manager, who is Jewish, comes just two days before Israel's general election, in which polls show a tight race between Prime Minister Benjamin Netanyahu's coalition and the opposition.

### ΓÇÿA hidden traditionΓÇÖ: Benjamin Moser wants you to know Jewish anti-Zionism is not new
- **Source**: Middle East and north Africa | The Guardian
- **Author**: Aaron Gell
- **Date**: 2026-10-25 12:00
- **Link**: https://www.theguardian.com/world/2026/oct/25/benjamin-moser-jewish-anti-zionism-book
- **Summary**: The writer rarely questioned the tenets of liberal Zionism growing up. Now he embraces anti-Zionism with the fervor of a new convertIn 1987, the Israeli human rights attorney Felicia Langer was paying a visit to the Neve Tirza womenΓÇÖs prison when a client named Siham shared some disturbing news: the prisoners held there had recently been sprayed with a chemical agent that left them writhing on the floor. Langer grabbed a tissue that had been exposed to the substance ΓÇô a piece of evidence, perhaps, in a future legal case. Then, on impulse, she lifted it to her face and inhaled deeply. Her hope, she wrote later, was that doing so might enable a doctor to identify the toxin.This anecdote appears in Anti-Zionism: A Jewish History, by Benjamin Moser. In it, the Pulitzer prize-winning author profiles a dozen Jews who actively opposed Zionism, before and after the establishment of Israel ΓÇô from Aaron Samuel Tamares, a Polish rabbi who began inveighing against Jewish nationalism in the late 19th century; to British diplomat Edwin Montagu, who sought to derail the 1917 Balfour declaration promising a Jewish state in Palestine; to Israeli ΓÇ£new historianΓÇ¥ Avi Shlaim ΓÇô figures he sees as part of ΓÇ£a hidden tradition ΓÇª of people who, though long dismissed and despised, were ultimately proven rightΓÇ¥. Along the way, he seeks to dismantle the Zionist ΓÇ£fairytale,ΓÇ¥ as he puts it, piece by piece. Continue reading...

### Riyadh airport resumes flights following Houthi attacks
- **Source**: AL-MONITOR: The Pulse of The Middle East
- **Author**: 
- **Date**: 2026-10-25 03:30
- **Link**: https://www.al-monitor.com/originals/2026/10/riyadh-airport-resumes-flights-following-houthi-attacks
- **Summary**: Riyadh's international airport said flights would resume on Sunday after a two-week closure following strikes from Yemen's Houthis on the Saudi capital, though the Iran-backed group warned of more to come. The Flight Radar monitoring site showed a plane taking off from Riyadh airport early Sunday, heading to the Saudi city of Jeddah. Saudi Arabia, along with Yemen's internationally recognised government that it backs, has faced a wave of Houthi attacks in recent weeks after a years-long truce broke down in the summer.

### Saudis say Riyadh airport partly reopens despite renewed Houthi threats
- **Source**: AL-MONITOR: The Pulse of The Middle East
- **Author**: 
- **Date**: 2026-10-25 00:46
- **Link**: https://www.al-monitor.com/originals/2026/10/saudis-say-riyadh-airport-partly-reopens-despite-renewed-houthi-threats
- **Summary**: Oct 25 (Reuters) - Saudi Arabia said its main airport resumed operations at two terminals on Sunday even as Yemen's Iran-aligned Houthis vowed to keep striking the facility, which they called a "military target".King Khalid International Airport in Riyadh had halted operations on October 10 following an attack claimed by the Houthis that killed 13 people, the deadliest in the kingdom in more than a decade.Saudi Arabia said it intercepted another wave of missiles and drones on Sunday as fighting with the militant group continued unabated.

- **Summary**: The prime minister has defined and dominated his countryΓÇÖs politics for three decades and is putting his rivals on the defensive before TuesdayΓÇÖs general electionThey chant his name, they cheer and clap ΓÇô and yet he is not the most dominant person in the room. Looming over the crowd that has gathered to hear Gadi Eisenkot, the former general who is the lead opposition challenger in TuesdayΓÇÖs Israeli election, is a man who is not there. It is the man Eisenkot seeks to replace as prime minister, the man who first served in that role three decades ago and who has held it, on and off, for 19 of the past 30 years. It is Benjamin Netanyahu.Naturally, the warm-up speakers at this evening rally in the modest city of ModiΓÇÖin, halfway between Tel Aviv and Jerusalem, stress the virtues of Eisenkot, a son of working-class Moroccan Jews who rose through the ranks to become chief of staff of the Israel Defense Forces and entered politics only four years ago. But every sentence might as well end with two words that go unspoken: unlike Bibi. Continue reading...
